# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- fixtures
_STATE = {}


def folder():
    if "folder" not in _STATE:
        f, got = C.fresh_snapshot(REPO, REV)
        _STATE["folder"], _STATE["commit"] = f, got
    return _STATE["folder"]


def readme():
    if "readme" not in _STATE:
        # CARD_README_OVERRIDE: a mutated copy of the card for the negative controls (test_card_controls.py); the
        # files under test stay the commit's
        p = C.REPORT.read(C.env("CARD_README_OVERRIDE") or os.path.join(folder(), "README.md"))
        _STATE["readme"] = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
        _STATE["blocks"] = C.readme_blocks(_STATE["readme"])
    return _STATE["readme"]


def blocks(lang=None, heading_contains=None, code_contains=None):
    readme()
    out = _STATE["blocks"]
    if lang:
        out = [b for b in out if b["lang"] == lang]
    if heading_contains:
        out = [b for b in out if heading_contains.lower() in b["heading"].lower()]
    if code_contains:
        out = [b for b in out if code_contains in b["code"]]
    return out


def hub_files():
    if "hub_files" not in _STATE:
        from huggingface_hub import HfApi
        info = HfApi().model_info(REPO, revision=REV or _STATE.get("commit"), files_metadata=True)
        _STATE["hub_files"] = {s.rfilename: (s.lfs.sha256 if s.lfs else None, s.size) for s in info.siblings}
        _STATE["hub_commit"] = info.sha
    return _STATE["hub_files"]


def paths_map():
    """The card's relative names -> the snapshot folder (every command runs from the folder's parent)."""
    return {"h2o-lightning-4b/": folder() + "/", "./h2o-lightning-4b": folder(), "cd h2o-lightning-4b": "cd " + folder()}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- static tier
@pytest.mark.static
class StaticTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_commit_is_the_one_asked_for(self):
        folder()
        if REV:
            self.assertTrue(C.HEX40.match(REV), "CARD_REV must be a full 40-hex commit")
            self.assertEqual(hub_files() and _STATE["hub_commit"], REV)

    def test_every_file_the_card_names_exists(self):
        names = C.referenced_paths(readme())
        present = set(os.path.basename(f) for f in hub_files()) | set(hub_files())
        local = set(os.listdir(folder()))
        # not shipped by design: label_rows.safetensors is the stale file the card tells users to delete,
        # .model_sha256.json is the sidecar the shim writes beside the weights at first use
        runtime = {"label_rows.safetensors", ".model_sha256.json", "results.jsonl",
                   "runs/text/score/index.json", "runs/vision/score/vision-scores.json"}   # outputs the kit writes
        missing = [n for n in names if n not in present and n not in local and n not in runtime]
        C.REPORT.result("files_named", names)
        self.assertEqual(missing, [])

    def test_pins_and_tags_resolve(self):
        from huggingface_hub import HfApi
        api = HfApi()
        refs = api.list_repo_refs(REPO)
        tags = {t.name for t in refs.tags}
        named = set(re.findall(r"`(v1\.\d(?:\.\d)*(?:\.\d)?)`", readme())) | set(re.findall(r"--revision (v1[\w.]+)", readme()))
        named = {n.rstrip("`;,).") for n in named}
        missing = sorted(n for n in named if n not in tags)
        C.REPORT.result("tags_named", sorted(named))
        self.assertEqual(missing, [], f"tags named on the card but absent on the Hub: {missing}")
        for rev in set(re.findall(r"--revision ([0-9a-f]{40})", readme())):
            self.assertEqual(api.model_info(BASE_REPO, revision=rev).sha, rev)

    def test_layout_only_the_weights_carry_a_weight_suffix(self):
        top = sorted(f for f in hub_files() if "/" not in f and f.endswith((".safetensors", ".bin", ".pt")))
        self.assertEqual(top, ["model.safetensors"])
        self.assertIn("label_rows.lightning", hub_files())
        self.assertNotIn("label_rows.safetensors", hub_files())

    def test_sibling_pins_match_the_shipped_files(self):
        shim = open(C.REPORT.read(os.path.join(folder(), "h2o_lightning_shim.py")), encoding="utf-8").read()
        pins = dict(re.findall(r"'([\w.]+\.py)': '([0-9a-f]{64})'", shim.split("SIBLING_SHA256 = ", 1)[1].split("\n", 1)[0]))
        self.assertEqual(set(pins), {"box.py", "assets.py", "pooled_readout.py"})
        for name, want in pins.items():
            self.assertEqual(C.sha256_file(C.REPORT.read(os.path.join(folder(), name))), want, name)

    def test_weights_are_not_the_base_model(self):
        if not os.path.isfile(os.path.join(folder(), "model.safetensors")):
            self.skipTest("model.safetensors not downloaded")
        name, frac = C.weights_differ_from_base(folder(), BASE_REPO, BASE_REV)
        C.REPORT.result("weights_vs_base", {"tensor": name, "equal_fraction": frac})
        self.assertLess(frac, 0.5)

    def test_json_blocks_parse_and_examples_pair_up(self):
        js = blocks("json")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(js), 2)
        for b in js:
            json.loads(b["code"])
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(example_pairs()), 1)   # the ticket example; the image example is a python block

    def test_curl_bodies_parse(self):
        bodies = [x for b in blocks("bash") for x in C.curl_json_bodies(b["code"])]
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(bodies), 3)
        for url, body in bodies:
            self.assertTrue(url and url.startswith("http://127.0.0.1:"), url)
            self.assertTrue("questions" in body or "messages" in body or "prompt" in body)

    def test_commands_use_flags_the_scripts_accept(self):
        serve = open(C.REPORT.read(os.path.join(folder(), "serve.sh")), encoding="utf-8").read()
        shim = open(os.path.join(folder(), "h2o_lightning_shim.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
        for b in blocks("bash"):
            for var in re.findall(r"\b([A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{3,})=", b["code"]):
                if var in ("PYTHONPATH", "HF_TOKEN"):
                    continue
                self.assertTrue(var in serve or var in shim or var.startswith("VLLM_"), f"{var} is not read by serve.sh or the shim")
            if "h2o_lightning_shim.py" in b["code"]:
                for flag in re.findall(r"(--[a-z-]+)", b["code"].split("h2o_lightning_shim.py", 1)[1]):
                    self.assertIn(f'"{flag}"', shim, flag)

    def run_serve(self, env, fake_build=False, fake_shim=None, timeout=120):
        """serve.sh (CARD_SERVE_OVERRIDE: a mutated copy, for the negative controls), copied with the package's engine/
        and configs into a scratch folder, with no real vLLM on PATH, so nothing can start a GPU engine: (return code,
        output). fake_build: a stand-in vLLM package that has the per-request attention entry point but none of the
        build's files (the sha256 check must refuse it), and a `vllm` command that exits at once. fake_shim: the source
        of a stand-in h2o_lightning_shim.py; the `vllm` command then stays up (a minute) instead of exiting."""
        serve = C.env("CARD_SERVE_OVERRIDE") or os.path.join(folder(), "serve.sh")
        work = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(folder()), "card_serve_check"); shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)
        pkg, bindir, site = (os.path.join(work, d) for d in ("pkg", "bin", "site"))
        for d in (pkg, bindir):
            os.makedirs(d)
        shutil.copy(serve, os.path.join(pkg, "serve.sh"))
        shutil.copytree(os.path.join(folder(), "engine"), os.path.join(pkg, "engine"))
        for f in ("config.json", "serve_config.json"):
            shutil.copy(os.path.join(folder(), f), pkg)
        py = os.path.join(bindir, "python3")
        with open(os.path.join(bindir, "vllm"), "w") as g:     # a `vllm` that exits at once, found first on PATH
            g.write("#!/bin/sh\nexec sleep 60 > /dev/null 2>&1\n" if fake_shim else "#!/bin/sh\nexit 1\n")
        if fake_shim:
            with open(os.path.join(pkg, "h2o_lightning_shim.py"), "w") as g:
                g.write(fake_shim)
        os.chmod(os.path.join(bindir, "vllm"), 0o755)
        with open(py, "w") as f:
            if fake_build:
                os.makedirs(os.path.join(site, "vllm", "v1"))
                for p, src in (("vllm/__init__.py", ""), ("vllm/v1/__init__.py", ""),
                               ("vllm/v1/request.py", "def parse_attention_mode(sampling_params):\n    return 'causal'\n")):
                    with open(os.path.join(site, p), "w") as g:
                        g.write(src)
                f.write(f'#!/bin/sh\nPYTHONPATH={site} exec {shutil.which("python3")} -S "$@"\n')
            else:
                f.write(f'#!/bin/sh\nexec {shutil.which("python3")} -I -S "$@"\n')
        os.chmod(py, 0o755)
        path = [bindir] + [d for d in os.environ.get("PATH", "").split(os.pathsep) if d]
        e = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k not in ("TWO_WAY", "ONE_ENGINE", "ATTENTION", "VIRTUAL_ENV", "PYTHONPATH")}
        e.update(env, PATH=os.pathsep.join(path))
        r = subprocess.run(["bash", os.path.join(pkg, "serve.sh")], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout, cwd=work, env=e)
        return r.returncode, r.stdout + r.stderr

    def test_two_way_without_one_engine_is_refused(self):
        """The two-engine two-way setup is gone: TWO_WAY=1 ONE_ENGINE=0 stops at once with the reason."""
        rc, out = self.run_serve({"TWO_WAY": "1", "ONE_ENGINE": "0"})
        C.REPORT.result("serve_two_engine_refused", {"rc": rc, "out": out[-300:]})
        self.assertNotEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertIn("no longer supported", out)

    def test_two_way_on_stock_vllm_is_refused(self):
        """The default (two-way, one engine) on a vLLM without per-request attention stops before vLLM starts."""
        rc, out = self.run_serve({})
        C.REPORT.result("serve_stock_refused", {"rc": rc, "out": out[-300:]})
        self.assertNotEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertIn("needs the vLLM build with per-request attention", out)

    def test_two_way_on_a_mismatched_build_is_refused(self):
        """A vLLM that has the per-request attention entry point but whose files do not match
        engine/vllm_per_request_attention.SHA256SUMS is refused before vLLM starts, on every start."""
        rc, out = self.run_serve({}, fake_build=True)
        C.REPORT.result("serve_mismatch_refused", {"rc": rc, "out": out[-300:]})
        self.assertNotEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertIn("does not match engine/vllm_per_request_attention.SHA256SUMS", out)

    def test_a_refused_tokenizer_stops_serve(self):
        """A vLLM without the training tokenizer (for example a download whose tokenizer/ folder is missing): the shim's
        /health answers 503 with its tokenizer_contract.json reason on every retry, so serve.sh stops with that reason
        instead of waiting for ever on /health?live=1."""
        import socket
        ports = []
        for _ in range(2):
            with socket.socket() as so:
                so.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)); ports.append(so.getsockname()[1])
        reason = ("vLLM's tokenizer splits 8 of 24 tokenizer_contract.json probes differently from the training "
                  "tokenizer (Qwen2Tokenizer): ['unicode-05']; start vLLM with --tokenizer <the model folder>/tokenizer")
        shim = ("import http.server, json, sys\n"
                "port = int(sys.argv[sys.argv.index('--port') + 1])\n"
                "class H(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n"
                "    def do_GET(self):\n"
                f"        b = json.dumps({{'ok': False, 'error': {reason!r}}}).encode()\n"
                "        self.send_response(503); self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(b))); self.end_headers()\n"
                "        self.wfile.write(b)\n"
                "    def log_message(self, *a):\n"
                "        pass\n"
                "http.server.HTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', port), H).serve_forever()\n")
        t0 = time.time()
        rc, out = self.run_serve({"TWO_WAY": "0", "PORT": str(ports[0]), "VLLM_PORT": str(ports[1])}, fake_shim=shim, timeout=45)
        C.REPORT.result("serve_tokenizer_refused", {"rc": rc, "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "out": out[-300:]})
        self.assertNotEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertIn("the shim refuses this vLLM", out)
        self.assertIn("tokenizer_contract.json", out)

    def test_the_build_list_is_the_pinned_one(self):
        """engine/vllm_per_request_attention.SHA256SUMS: 30 files under vllm/, all Python, each line a sha256 and a path."""
        with open(os.path.join(folder(), "engine", "vllm_per_request_attention.SHA256SUMS"), encoding="utf-8") as f:
            rows = [l.split() for l in f if l.strip()]
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 30)
        for sha, path in rows:
            self.assertRegex(sha, r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
            self.assertTrue(path.startswith("vllm/") and path.endswith(".py"), path)
        self.assertIn("b05cc29ab813895947954c268c4ec41ca0da1306", readme())

    def test_serve_reports_ready_only_after_the_full_warm_up(self):
        """serve.sh warms every path the card uses before /health turns ready and the ready line prints: both modes on
        the one engine, a three-question request (choice, yes/no, score), a long record and an image; the shim is
        started with SHIM_READY_FILE and the flag is written only after the warm-up."""
        serve = open(C.env("CARD_SERVE_OVERRIDE") or os.path.join(folder(), "serve.sh"), encoding="utf-8").read()
        shim = open(os.path.join(folder(), "h2o_lightning_shim.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
        warm = serve[serve.index("warm() {"):serve.index('echo "H2O-Lightning-4B ready')]
        for needle in ('"attention_mode": "bidirectional"', '"attention_mode": "causal"', '\\"type\\": \\"score\\"',
                       '\\"type\\": \\"choice\\"', '$LONG', "data:image/png", ': > "$SHIM_READY_FILE"'):
            self.assertIn(needle, warm, needle)
        self.assertLess(serve.rindex('    warm "{'), serve.index(': > "$SHIM_READY_FILE"'))   # flag after the last warm-up
        self.assertLess(serve.index('export SHIM_READY_FILE'), serve.index('h2o_lightning_shim.py" --config'))
        self.assertIn('/health?live=1', serve)
        self.assertIn('SHIM_READY_FILE', shim)

    def test_the_card_describes_only_this_release(self):
        """The card describes only this release: no earlier-release sections, numbers or pins (older versions live in the
        repo's tags); one neutral pointer to them."""
        text = readme()
        for word in ("v1.3.1", "v1.3.0", "v1.2.3", "Earlier release", "earlier release", "## JevBench\n", "#1 on JevBench"):
            self.assertNotIn(word, text)
        self.assertIn("Older versions: see the repo's tags.", text)

    def test_no_comparison_with_other_vendors_models_by_name(self):
        """The card compares only H2O-Lightning releases with each other (and the benchmark's own reference model)."""
        import hashlib
        banned = {"4b1ef08fd267928e19c3038c4a4952d865dc2153a25db9b3bd493c126a8bf946"}   # sha256 of one lowercased vendor product name
        words = set(re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", readme().lower()))
        self.assertEqual([w for w in words if hashlib.sha256(w.encode()).hexdigest() in banned], [])

    def test_links_resolve(self):
        import urllib.request
        if C.env("CARD_OFFLINE"):
            self.skipTest("CARD_OFFLINE")
        # a pip VCS pin (git+https://...@<commit>) is checked as its repository URL
        urls = sorted(set(re.sub(r"@[0-9a-f]{7,40}$", "", u.rstrip(".,)"))
                          for u in re.findall(r"https?://[^\s)>\]`\"']+", readme()) if "127.0.0.1" not in u))
        bad = {}
        for u in urls:
            if "wheels.vllm.ai" in u or "download.pytorch.org" in u:
                continue  # index URLs answer 403/404 to a bare GET
            try:
                req = urllib.request.Request(u, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (card-test)"}, method="GET")
                with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as r:
                    if r.status >= 400:
                        bad[u] = r.status
            except Exception as e:
                bad[u] = str(e)[:80]
        C.REPORT.result("links", {"checked": len(urls), "bad": bad})
        self.assertEqual(bad, {})

    def test_in_card_anchors_resolve(self):
        """Every (#anchor) link in the card names a heading of the card (Hugging Face's slug: lower case, punctuation
        dropped, spaces to hyphens); headings inside fenced blocks do not count."""
        prose = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", readme(), flags=re.S)
        slugs = {re.sub(r"[^\w\- ]", "", h.strip().lower()).replace(" ", "-") for h in re.findall(r"^#{1,6}[ \t]+(.+)$", prose, re.M)}
        anchors = set(re.findall(r"\]\(#([^)\s]+)\)", prose))
        self.assertTrue(anchors)
        self.assertEqual(sorted(anchors - slugs), [])

    def test_registered_claims_are_in_the_card(self):
        missing = C.claims_check(readme(), CLAIMS)
        self.assertEqual(missing, [])

    def test_no_placeholder_tokens_in_the_card(self):
        """A card under preparation may carry __TOKEN__ placeholders for numbers still being measured; none may ship."""
        found = sorted(set(re.findall(r"__[A-Z0-9_]+__", readme())))
        self.assertEqual(found, [], f"placeholder tokens in README.md: {found}")

    def test_numeric_sentences_are_listed_in_the_report(self):
        """Every numeric sentence of the measured sections goes to the report; the registered ones are marked, so a new
        number on the card shows up as unregistered in the report for the reviewer."""
        rows = C.numeric_sentences(readme(), {"Measured", "Two-way reading (recommended)"})
        reg = [(s, l, any(c["quote"] in l or l in c["quote"] for c in CLAIMS)) for s, l in rows]
        C.REPORT.result("numeric_sentences", [{"section": s, "line": l, "registered": r} for s, l, r in reg])
        self.assertGreater(len(rows), 5)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- live tier
def example_pairs():
    """(request json block, response json block) pairs: a json block followed by 'The response' and a json block."""
    rd = readme()
    pairs = []
    js = blocks("json")
    for a, b in zip(js, js[1:]):
        between = rd[a["pos"]:b["pos"]]
        req, resp = json.loads(a["code"]), json.loads(b["code"])
        # a request block holds "questions", a response block "answers"; a response followed by another response
        # (the python example's reply) is not a pair
        if "The response" in between and "questions" in req and "answers" in resp:
            pairs.append((req, resp))
    return pairs


class Served:
    """One launch command from the card, run as written from the snapshot folder's parent, stopped at the end."""

    def __init__(self, test, code, extra_env=None, ready="ready on http", health=None, seconds=1200, cwd=None):
        self.test = test
        cwd = cwd or os.path.dirname(folder())   # the card's commands run from the download's parent (h2o-lightning-4b/...)
        self.log = os.path.join(cwd, f"card_live_{int(time.time())}.log")
        code = C.substitute(code, paths_map())
        self.proc = C.run_card_command(code, cwd, self.log, extra_env)
        ok = C.wait_log(self.log, ready, seconds, self.proc) if ready else C.wait_http(health, seconds, self.proc)
        if not ok:
            tail = open(self.log, "rb").read()[-3000:].decode("utf-8", "replace")
            C.stop_tree(self.proc)
            test.fail(f"the card's command did not come up: {code[:120]}\n{tail}")

    def stop(self):
        C.stop_tree(self.proc)
        C.REPORT.read(self.log)


def assert_answer_shape(test, got, shown, tol=TOL_PROB):
    """The served answer against the card's shown answer: same keys, same types, same choice, numbers within tol."""
    test.assertEqual(set(got["answers"]), set(shown["answers"]))
    for q, s in shown["answers"].items():
        g = got["answers"][q]
        test.assertEqual(g["type"], s["type"], q)
        for k in s:
            test.assertIn(k, g, f"{q}.{k}")
        if "choice" in s:
            test.assertEqual(g["choice"], s["choice"], q)
        if "probabilities" in s:
            test.assertEqual(set(g["probabilities"]), set(s["probabilities"]))
            for k, v in s["probabilities"].items():
                test.assertAlmostEqual(g["probabilities"][k], v, delta=tol, msg=f"{q}.{k}")
        for k in ("noul", "score", "confidence"):
            if k in s:
                test.assertAlmostEqual(g[k], s[k], delta=tol * (2 if k == "score" else 1), msg=f"{q}.{k}")
    for k in ("probability_source", "effort_used"):
        test.assertEqual(got[k], shown[k], k)
    for k in shown["usage"]:
        test.assertIn(k, got["usage"])


def ticket_block():
    return [b for b in blocks("bash") if "Which team should take it?" in b["code"] and "urgency" in b["code"]][0]


@pytest.mark.live
@pytest.mark.skipif(not C.env("CARD_LIVE"), reason="CARD_LIVE=1 runs the card's launch commands on a GPU")
class LiveTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def run_examples(self, mode):
        url, body = C.curl_json_bodies(ticket_block()["code"])[0]
        st, r = C.post_example(url, body)
        self.assertEqual(st, 200, r)
        self.assertEqual(r["attention_mode"], mode)
        self.assertEqual(set(r["answers"]), set(body["questions"]))
        results = {"ticket": r}
        for i, (req, shown) in enumerate(example_pairs()):
            st, got = C.post_example("http://127.0.0.1:8741/v1/systemone", req)
            self.assertEqual(st, 200, got)
            assert_answer_shape(self, got, shown)
            results[f"example_{i}"] = got
        img = [b for b in blocks("python") if "example_receipt.png" in b["code"]]
        if img:
            code = C.substitute(img[0]["code"], paths_map())
            out = subprocess.run(["python3", "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900, cwd=os.path.dirname(folder()))
            self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr[-500:])
            got = json.loads(out.stdout)
            # the card shows the reply as the json block after the python example ("The response (rounded here)")
            shown = [json.loads(b["code"]) for b in blocks("json") if "answers" in json.loads(b["code"]) and "matches" in json.loads(b["code"])["answers"]]
            self.assertEqual(len(shown), 1, "the card must show exactly one reply for the receipt example")
            assert_answer_shape(self, got, shown[0])
            results["example_image"] = got
        return results

    def build_env(self):
        """PATH with CARD_ONE_ENGINE_VLLM (a `vllm` executable of the per-request-attention build) first; the two-way
        lines need it (serve.sh refuses a stock vLLM)."""
        wrapper = C.env("CARD_ONE_ENGINE_VLLM")
        if not wrapper:
            self.skipTest("CARD_ONE_ENGINE_VLLM (the per-request-attention vLLM build) runs the card's two-way lines")
        bindir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(folder()), "card_one_engine_bin"); os.makedirs(bindir, exist_ok=True)
        for name, target in (("vllm", wrapper), ("python3", os.path.join(os.path.dirname(wrapper), "python3"))):
            link = os.path.join(bindir, name)
            if os.path.lexists(link):
                os.remove(link)
            if os.path.exists(target):
                os.symlink(target, link)
        return {"PATH": bindir + os.pathsep + os.environ["PATH"]}

    def launch_block(self, needle):
        bs = [b for b in blocks("bash") if needle in b["code"] and "serve.sh" in b["code"]]
        self.assertTrue(bs, needle)
        line = [l for l in bs[0]["code"].splitlines() if needle in l][0].split("#")[0].strip()
        return line

    # numbered: unittest runs methods in name order
    def test_1_default_two_way_serves_the_examples(self):
        """The card's default line on the build: one engine answers two-way by default and one-way per request, and
        the two modes differ on the ticket (an engine ignoring the flag would not)."""
        s = Served(self, self.launch_block("bash h2o-lightning-4b/serve.sh"), extra_env=self.build_env())
        try:
            self.assertIn("one engine", open(s.log, "rb").read().decode("utf-8", "replace"))
            C.REPORT.result("live_two_way", self.run_examples("bidirectional"))
            url, body = C.curl_json_bodies(ticket_block()["code"])[0]
            got = {}
            for mode in ("bidirectional", "causal"):
                st, r = C.post_example(url, dict(body, attention_mode=mode))
                self.assertEqual(st, 200, (mode, r))
                self.assertEqual(r["attention_mode"], mode)
                got[mode] = r
            two, one = got["bidirectional"]["answers"]["outage"]["noul"], got["causal"]["answers"]["outage"]["noul"]
            self.assertGreater(abs(two - one), 0.005, "the flagged and unflagged reads gave the same answer")
            C.REPORT.result("live_one_engine", got)
        finally:
            s.stop()

    def test_2_one_way_serves_the_examples(self):
        s = Served(self, self.launch_block("TWO_WAY=0 bash"))
        try:
            C.REPORT.result("live_one_way", self.run_examples("causal"))
            url, body = C.curl_json_bodies(ticket_block()["code"])[0]
            st, r = C.post_example(url, dict(body, attention_mode="bidirectional"))
            self.assertEqual(st, 400, "a two-way request on the one-way fallback must get 400")
        finally:
            s.stop()

    def test_3_box_on_the_one_engine(self):
        s = Served(self, self.launch_block("bash h2o-lightning-4b/serve.sh"), extra_env=self.build_env())
        try:
            self.assertIn("box questions by guided generation", open(s.log, "rb").read().decode("utf-8", "replace"))
            import base64
            png = base64.b64encode(open(os.path.join(folder(), "example_receipt.png"), "rb").read()).decode()
            st, r = C.post_example("http://127.0.0.1:8741/v1/systemone", {
                "assets": {"IMG_1": {"type": "image", "format": "png", "data_base64": png}}, "state": {"image": "IMG_1"},
                "questions": {"where": {"type": "box", "instructions": "the total amount line in IMG_1"}}})
            self.assertEqual(st, 200, r)
            a = r["answers"]["where"]
            self.assertEqual(set(a["box"]), {"left", "top", "right", "bottom"})
            self.assertTrue(0 <= a["confidence"] <= 1 and 0 <= a["present"] <= 1)
            C.REPORT.result("live_box", r)
        finally:
            s.stop()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- slow tier
@pytest.mark.slow
class SlowTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_bench231_public_items(self):
        kit = C.env("CARD_BENCH231_DIR")
        if not (kit and C.env("CARD_LIVE")):
            self.skipTest("CARD_BENCH231_DIR (the benchmark checkout at the card's commit) and CARD_LIVE=1 run the 231 items")
        # the card's harness block: the one with "cli run" and a --results flag (the benchmark's own command string stays as written)
        cmd = [b for b in blocks("bash") if "cli run" in b["code"] and "--results" in b["code"]][0]["code"]
        results = {}
        for mode, launch, claim in (("two-way", "bash h2o-lightning-4b/serve.sh", CLAIMS[0]),):   # the two-way row (one-way: a static quote)
            line = [l for l in [b for b in blocks("bash") if launch in b["code"] and "serve.sh" in b["code"]][0]["code"].splitlines() if launch in l][0].split("#")[0].strip()
            s = Served(self, line, extra_env=LiveTest.build_env(self))
            try:
                out = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(folder()), f"card_bench231_{mode}")
                shutil.rmtree(out, ignore_errors=True); os.makedirs(out)
                code = re.sub(r"<[A-Z_]+>", kit, cmd, count=1)   # the card's first placeholder: the checkout path
                code = code.replace("<OUT>", out)                 # the card's output placeholder (results, raw, ledger, manifest)
                r = subprocess.run(["bash", "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=7200, cwd=kit)
                self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stderr[-800:])
                rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(os.path.join(out, "results.jsonl"))]
                correct = sum(bool(x.get("correct")) for x in rows)
                results[mode] = {"rows": len(rows), "correct": correct, "claim": claim["correct"]}
                self.assertEqual(len(rows), 231)
                self.assertLessEqual(abs(correct - claim["correct"]), claim["tolerance"], results)
            finally:
                s.stop()
        C.REPORT.result("bench231", results)

    def test_decision_index_public_suite(self):
        self.skipTest("the Decision Index 0.3 public suite (140,178 text rows, 12,210 vision rows) takes hours per mode; "
                      "run the kit's own scorer at the card's settings (max_state_tokens 1000000), on a vision suite that passes "
                      "`decision-index vision verify`, and compare with the card's numbers within 0.3 (not yet run on this release)")



================================================
FILE: test_card_controls.py
================================================
"""Negative controls for the card suite: a mutated copy of README.md (or of serve.sh) must make the static tier FAIL,
so a suite that passes is known to be able to fail. Each control runs `pytest test_card.py -m static` in a subprocess
with CARD_README_OVERRIDE (or CARD_SERVE_OVERRIDE) pointing at the mutated copy (the commit's files are untouched) and
expects a non-zero exit with the named test among the failures.

    CARD_REV=<40-hex> CARD_DIR=<folder> pytest test_card_controls.py
"""
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

import pytest

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
CONTROLS = [
    # (name, mutation of the card text, the test that must fail)
    ("a registered number changed", lambda t: t.replace("**206 / 231 (89.2 %)**", "**208 / 231 (90.0 %)**", 1), "test_registered_claims_are_in_the_card"),
    ("a file the card names does not exist", lambda t: t.replace("`serve_config.json`", "`serve_config_v2.json`", 1), "test_every_file_the_card_names_exists"),
    ("a tag the card names does not exist", lambda t: t.replace("`--revision v1.4.1`", "`--revision v1.4.9`", 1), "test_pins_and_tags_resolve"),
    ("a json example made unparsable", lambda t: t.replace('"probabilities": {"p1": 0.948, "p2": 0.029, "p3": 0.023}},', '"probabilities": {"p1": 0.948, "p2": 0.029, "p3": 0.023},', 1), "test_json_blocks_parse_and_examples_pair_up"),
]
# (name, mutation of serve.sh, the test that must fail): the two-engine refusal removed must be noticed
SERVE_CONTROLS = [
    ("the two-engine refusal removed from serve.sh",
     lambda t: re.sub(r'if \[ "\$TWO_WAY" = 1 \] && \[ "\$ONE_ENGINE" = 0 \]; then\n.*?\nfi\n', "", t, count=1, flags=re.S),
     "test_two_way_without_one_engine_is_refused"),
    ("the build's sha256 check removed from serve.sh",
     lambda t: "\n".join(l for l in t.split("\n") if 'sha256sum -c --quiet "$SUMS"' not in l),
     "test_two_way_on_a_mismatched_build_is_refused"),
    ("the ready file written before the warm-up in serve.sh",
     lambda t: t.replace(': > "$SHIM_READY_FILE"\n', '', 1).replace('LONG="$(printf', ': > "$SHIM_READY_FILE"\nLONG="$(printf', 1),
     "test_serve_reports_ready_only_after_the_full_warm_up"),
    ("the stop on a refused tokenizer removed from serve.sh",
     lambda t: "\n".join(l for l in t.split("\n") if "*tokenizer_contract.json*)" not in l),
     "test_a_refused_tokenizer_stops_serve"),
]


@pytest.mark.static
class NegativeControls(unittest.TestCase):
    def run_static(self, text, serve_text=None):
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", suffix=".md", delete=False, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(text); path = f.name
        env = dict(os.environ, CARD_README_OVERRIDE=path, CARD_REPORT=path + ".json", CARD_OFFLINE=os.environ.get("CARD_OFFLINE", ""))
        sdir = None
        if serve_text is not None:
            sdir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            with open(os.path.join(sdir, "serve.sh"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(serve_text)
            env["CARD_SERVE_OVERRIDE"] = os.path.join(sdir, "serve.sh")
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", "test_card.py", "-m", "static"], cwd=HERE, env=env,
                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
        os.remove(path)
        if os.path.exists(path + ".json"):
            os.remove(path + ".json")
        if sdir:
            os.remove(os.path.join(sdir, "serve.sh")); os.rmdir(sdir)
        return r

    def test_the_unmutated_card_passes(self):
        folder = os.environ.get("CARD_DIR") or HERE
        text = open(os.path.join(folder, "README.md"), encoding="utf-8").read()
        r = self.run_static(text)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout[-1500:])

    def test_each_mutation_fails_the_named_test(self):
        folder = os.environ.get("CARD_DIR") or HERE
        text = open(os.path.join(folder, "README.md"), encoding="utf-8").read()
        for name, mutate, failing in CONTROLS:
            with self.subTest(control=name):
                mutated = mutate(text)
                self.assertNotEqual(mutated, text, f"the mutation '{name}' did not change the card: update the control")
                r = self.run_static(mutated)
                self.assertNotEqual(r.returncode, 0, f"the static tier passed on a mutated card ({name})")
                self.assertIn(failing, r.stdout, f"{failing} did not fail on '{name}':\n{r.stdout[-1200:]}")

    def test_each_serve_mutation_fails_the_named_test(self):
        folder = os.environ.get("CARD_DIR") or HERE
        text = open(os.path.join(folder, "README.md"), encoding="utf-8").read()
        serve = open(os.path.join(folder, "serve.sh"), encoding="utf-8").read()
        for name, mutate, failing in SERVE_CONTROLS:
            with self.subTest(control=name):
                mutated = mutate(serve)
                self.assertNotEqual(mutated, serve, f"the mutation '{name}' did not change serve.sh: update the control")
                r = self.run_static(text, mutated)
                self.assertNotEqual(r.returncode, 0, f"the static tier passed on a mutated serve.sh ({name})")
                self.assertIn(failing, r.stdout, f"{failing} did not fail on '{name}':\n{r.stdout[-1200:]}")



================================================
FILE: test_security.py
================================================
"""Image links are fetched safely by the shim (python3 -m unittest test_security): public https only, resolved addresses
checked and pinned, redirects re-checked, a hard deadline however slowly a server sends, size capped, links counted
before any is fetched, real images only, nothing kept; vLLM never fetches."""
import base64
import os
import socket
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from unittest import mock

import h2o_lightning_shim as S
PNG = base64.b64decode("iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mP8z8BQDwAEhQGAhKmMIQAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==")
PUBLIC_IP = "93.184.216.34"


class Origin(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def do_GET(self):
        if self.path.startswith("/png"):
            return self._send(200, "image/png", PNG)
        if self.path.startswith("/redirect-internal"):
            self.send_response(302)
            self.send_header("Location", "http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/")
            return self.end_headers()
        if self.path.startswith("/html"):
            return self._send(200, "text/html", b"<html></html>")
        if self.path.startswith("/fake"):
            return self._send(200, "image/png", b"not really an image")
        if self.path.startswith("/big"):
            return self._send(200, "image/png", PNG + b"\0" * (3 * 1024 * 1024))
        if self.path.startswith("/drip-headers"):  # one header byte every 0.2 s, for ever
            self.wfile.write(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\n")
            for _ in range(200):
                self.wfile.write(b"X")
                self.wfile.flush()
                time.sleep(0.2)
            return None
        if self.path.startswith("/drip-body"):  # one body byte every 0.2 s, for ever
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "image/png")
            self.end_headers()
            for b in PNG * 100:
                self.wfile.write(bytes([b]))
                self.wfile.flush()
                time.sleep(0.2)
            return None
        if self.path.startswith("/slow"):
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "image/png")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(PNG[:8])
            self.wfile.flush()
            time.sleep(3)
            return None
        return self._send(404, "text/plain", b"no")

    def _send(self, status, ctype, body):
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def handle(self):
        try:
            super().handle()
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
            pass

    def log_message(self, *args):
        pass


class SafeFetch(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Origin)
        cls.server.daemon_threads = True
        threading.Thread(target=cls.server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        cls.server.shutdown()
        cls.server.server_close()

    def setUp(self):
        real = socket.getaddrinfo
        port = self.server.server_port
        self.connected = []

        def fake(host, p, *a, **k):
            if host == "img.test":
                return [(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, "", (PUBLIC_IP, p))]
            if host == "slowdns.test":
                time.sleep(3)
                return [(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, "", (PUBLIC_IP, p))]
            if host == "rebind.test":
                return [(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, "", (PUBLIC_IP, p)),
                        (socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM, 6, "", ("10.1.2.3", p))]
            return real(host, p, *a, **k)

        test = self

        def connect(conn):
            test.connected.append(conn._ip)
            test.assertEqual(conn._ip, PUBLIC_IP)
            conn.raw = conn.sock = socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), conn.timeout)

        env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("LIGHTNING_IMAGE_")}
        env["LIGHTNING_IMAGE_URL_ALLOW_HTTP"] = "true"  # the local test origin speaks plain http
        for p in (mock.patch.object(socket, "getaddrinfo", fake), mock.patch.object(S._PinnedHTTP, "connect", connect),
                  mock.patch.dict(os.environ, env, clear=True)):
            p.start()
            self.addCleanup(p.stop)

    def test_internal_and_rebinding_targets_are_refused_without_connecting(self):
        for url in ("http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/", "http://10.0.0.5:6379/", "https://127.0.0.1/x.png",
                    "https://localhost/x.png", "https://[::1]/", "https://[fd00:ec2::254]/", "https://100.64.1.1/",
                    "https://[::ffff:10.0.0.1]/", "https://u:p@img.test/png", "ftp://img.test/png",
                    "https://rebind.test/png"):
            with self.subTest(url=url), self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable) as caught:
                S.image_from_string(url, True)
            self.assertNotIn("169.254", str(caught.exception))
        self.assertEqual(self.connected, [])

    def test_a_public_image_link_becomes_a_data_uri_and_leaves_no_trace(self):
        before = set(os.listdir(tempfile.gettempdir()))
        with self.assertLogs("lightning.image_fetch", "INFO") as logs:
            got = S.image_from_string("http://img.test/png?token=SECRETQ", True)
        self.assertEqual(got, "data:image/png;base64," + base64.b64encode(PNG).decode())
        text = "\n".join(logs.output)
        self.assertNotIn("SECRETQ", text)
        self.assertNotIn("img.test", text)
        self.assertEqual(set(os.listdir(tempfile.gettempdir())), before)

    def test_redirect_to_internal_non_images_oversize_and_slow_are_cut(self):
        with mock.patch.object(S, "IMAGE_FETCH_MAX_BYTES", 1024 * 1024), mock.patch.object(S, "IMAGE_FETCH_SECONDS", 1.0):
            for path in ("/redirect-internal", "/html", "/fake", "/big", "/slow", "/missing"):
                t0 = time.monotonic()
                with self.subTest(path=path), self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable):
                    S.image_from_string("http://img.test" + path, True)
                self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t0, 2.5)

    def test_the_deadline_holds_against_slow_names_and_dripping_headers_or_bodies(self):
        with mock.patch.object(S, "IMAGE_FETCH_SECONDS", 1.0):
            for url in ("http://img.test/drip-headers", "http://img.test/drip-body", "http://slowdns.test/png"):
                t0 = time.monotonic()
                with self.subTest(url=url), self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable):
                    S.image_from_string(url, True)
                self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t0, 2.0)

    def test_links_are_counted_before_any_is_fetched(self):
        body = {"state": "x", "images": ["http://img.test/png?%d" % i for i in range(50)]}
        with mock.patch.object(S, "_fetch_bytes", side_effect=AssertionError("fetched")) as fetch:
            with self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable) as caught:
                S.extract_images(body, 4)
        self.assertIn("50 images", str(caught.exception))
        self.assertEqual(fetch.call_count, 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.connected, [])
        state, images = S.extract_images({"state": "x", "images": ["http://img.test/png", "http://img.test/png"]}, 4)
        self.assertEqual(len(images), 2)
        self.assertTrue(all(i.startswith("data:image/png;base64,") for i in images))

    def test_a_request_total_size_cap_and_a_concurrency_cap(self):
        with mock.patch.object(S, "IMAGE_FETCH_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES", len(PNG) + 10):
            with self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable):
                S.extract_images({"state": "x", "images": ["http://img.test/png"] * 3}, 4)
        self.connected.clear()
        busy = threading.BoundedSemaphore(1)  # every fetch slot taken: the next link waits at most the deadline
        busy.acquire()
        with mock.patch.object(S, "_FETCH_SLOTS", busy), mock.patch.object(S, "IMAGE_FETCH_SECONDS", 0.3):
            t0 = time.monotonic()
            with self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable):
                S.image_from_string("http://img.test/png", True)
            self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t0, 1.0)
        self.assertEqual(self.connected, [])

    def test_embedded_ipv4_forms_idn_deny_names_ports_and_non_ascii_paths(self):
        for text in ("::127.0.0.1", "::a00:1", "::ffff:0:a00:1", "::ffff:127.0.0.1"):
            self.assertFalse(S._public_address(text), text)
        with mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"LIGHTNING_IMAGE_URL_DENY_HOSTS": "img.test"}):
            with self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable):
                S.image_from_string("http://\uff49mg.test/png", True)  # a full-width spelling of img.test
        for url in ("http://img.test:8080/png", "https://img.test:8443/png"):
            with self.subTest(url=url), self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable):
                S.image_from_string(url, True)
        self.assertEqual(self.connected, [])
        got = S.image_from_string("http://img.test/png/caf\u00e9 \u5199\u771f.png?q=\u00e9", True)
        self.assertTrue(got.startswith("data:image/png;base64,"))

    def test_switch_off_and_plain_http_needs_opt_in(self):
        with mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"LIGHTNING_IMAGE_URLS": "off"}):
            with self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable):
                S.image_from_string("http://img.test/png", True)
        with mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"LIGHTNING_IMAGE_URL_ALLOW_HTTP": "false"}):
            with self.assertRaises(S.Unprocessable):
                S.image_from_string("http://img.test/png", True)

    def test_a_link_inside_a_record_stays_text(self):
        self.assertIsNone(S.image_from_string("https://example.com/a.png", False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()



================================================
FILE: test_shim.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Tests for h2o_lightning_shim.py against a stand-in for vLLM. No GPU, no network, standard library only.

    python3 test_shim.py

The stand-in speaks vLLM's /v1/models, /tokenize, /detokenize and /v1/completions (with `logprob_token_ids`) over
a toy tokenizer; a second stand-in speaks the pooling runner's /v1/embeddings for the two-way attention tests. The tests check the prompt, the answers (temperature, yes/no floor, score), several questions per
request, more options than one vLLM request reads, state truncation, the label check, and the status codes
an evaluation runner depends on.
"""
import base64
import copy
import json
import math
import os
import re
import sys
import threading
import unittest
from unittest import mock
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
import h2o_lightning_shim as S
REAL_TOKENIZER_CONTRACT = S.TOKENIZER_CONTRACT
S.TOKENIZER_CONTRACT = None   # the stand-in engine has its own vocabulary; TokenizerContractTest checks the contract

CFG = json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "serve_config.json")))
CFG.pop("image", None)            # the text-path tests use the config as a text deployment; ImageTest adds its own block
if isinstance(CFG["temperature"], dict):   # the tests use one T; test_temperature_by_type covers the per-type form
    CFG["temperature"] = CFG["temperature"]["choice"]
PIECE = re.compile(r" ?[^\s]+|\s+")


class FakeVLLM(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    """Label j's log-probability is -0.3 * j unless the prompt carries MOCK_LP=[...]; MOCK_OVERFLOW answers vLLM's
    context error and MOCK_500 a server error."""
    vocab, inv, prompts, completions, lock = {}, {}, [], 0, threading.Lock()

    @classmethod
    def reset(cls):
        cls.vocab = {" " + l: 1000 + j for j, l in enumerate(CFG["labels"])}
        cls.inv = {i: p for p, i in cls.vocab.items()}
        cls.prompts, cls.completions = [], 0
        cls.chats, cls.skew = [], False
        cls.flagged, cls.flag_twist = [], 2.0        # the per-request attention flag (the one-engine fork)
        cls.writes, cls.tokenizes = [], 0             # (prompt, vllm_xargs prefix_cache_write); /tokenize calls
        cls.no_token_ids = False                      # an engine that does not return prompt_token_ids

    @classmethod
    def ids(cls, text):
        out = []
        with cls.lock:
            for p in PIECE.findall(text):
                if p not in cls.vocab:
                    cls.vocab[p] = 500000 + len(cls.vocab)
                    cls.inv[cls.vocab[p]] = p
                out.append(cls.vocab[p])
        return out

    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def _send(self, code, obj):
        b = json.dumps(obj).encode()
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(b)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(b)

    def do_GET(self):
        self._send(200, {"object": "list", "data": [{"id": CFG["model"], "max_model_len": 40960}]})

    def do_POST(self):
        req = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
        if self.path == "/tokenize" and "messages" in req:
            assert req["continue_final_message"] and not req["add_generation_prompt"]
            return self._send(200, {"tokens": self.ids(self.render(req["messages"]))})
        if self.path == "/tokenize":
            with FakeVLLM.lock:
                FakeVLLM.tokenizes += 1
            return self._send(200, {"tokens": self.ids(req["prompt"])})
        if self.path == "/v1/chat/completions":
            return self.chat(req)
        if self.path == "/detokenize":
            return self._send(200, {"prompt": "".join(self.inv[i] for i in req["tokens"])})
        text = req["prompt"]
        with FakeVLLM.lock:
            FakeVLLM.prompts.append(text)
            FakeVLLM.completions += 1
        if "MOCK_OVERFLOW" in text:
            return self._send(400, {"error": {"message": "This model's maximum context length is 40960 tokens."}})
        if "MOCK_500" in text:
            return self._send(500, {"error": {"message": "boom"}})
        ids = req["logprob_token_ids"]
        assert req["logprobs"] == len(ids) <= 128 and req["max_tokens"] == 1 and req["return_tokens_as_token_ids"]
        m = re.search(r"MOCK_LP=(\[[^\]]*\])", text)
        lp = json.loads(m.group(1)) if m else None
        twist = FakeVLLM.flag_twist if (req.get("vllm_xargs") or {}).get("attention_mode") == "bidirectional" else 0.0
        with FakeVLLM.lock:
            FakeVLLM.flagged.append((req.get("vllm_xargs") or {}).get("attention_mode"))
            FakeVLLM.writes.append((text, (req.get("vllm_xargs") or {}).get("prefix_cache_write")))
        top = {f"token_id:{i}": (lp[i - 1000] if lp else -0.3 * (i - 1000)) - (twist if i == 1000 else 0.0) for i in ids}
        choice = {"text": "", "logprobs": {"top_logprobs": [top]}}
        if req.get("return_token_ids") and not FakeVLLM.no_token_ids:
            choice["prompt_token_ids"] = self.ids(text)
        self._send(200, {"choices": [choice], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": len(self.ids(text)), "completion_tokens": 1}})


    # ---- the image path: chat completions, rendered as Qwen3.5's template renders a continued final assistant turn
    IMAGE_TOKENS = 9                    # each image's pad becomes this many tokens
    chats, skew = [], False             # skew: render the assistant turn wrongly (a template that is not the model's)

    @classmethod
    def render(cls, messages):
        out = ""
        for i, m in enumerate(messages):
            c = m["content"]
            if isinstance(c, list):
                c = "".join(S.VISION_SLOT if p["type"] == "image_url" else p["text"] for p in c)
            c = c.strip()
            if m["role"] == "assistant":
                assert i == len(messages) - 1
                out += ("<|im_start|>assistant\n" if cls.skew else "<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\n") + c
            else:
                out += f"<|im_start|>{m['role']}\n{c}<|im_end|>\n"
        return out

    def chat(self, req):
        text = self.render(req["messages"])
        n_img = sum(p["type"] == "image_url" for m in req["messages"] if isinstance(m["content"], list)
                    for p in m["content"])
        with FakeVLLM.lock:
            FakeVLLM.chats.append(req)
        if any(p.get("image_url", {}).get("url", "").endswith("MOCK_422.png") for m in req["messages"]
               if isinstance(m["content"], list) for p in m["content"]):
            return self._send(422, {"error": {"message": "Failed to fetch the image"}})
        ids = req["logprob_token_ids"]
        assert req["logprobs"] is True and len(ids) <= 128 and req["max_completion_tokens"] == 1
        assert req["continue_final_message"] and not req["add_generation_prompt"] and req["return_tokens_as_token_ids"]
        assert req["chat_template_kwargs"] == {"enable_thinking": False} and req["temperature"] == 0.0
        m = re.search(r"MOCK_LP=(\[[^\]]*\])", text)
        lp = json.loads(m.group(1)) if m else None
        twist = FakeVLLM.flag_twist if (req.get("vllm_xargs") or {}).get("attention_mode") == "bidirectional" else 0.0
        top = [{"token": f"token_id:{i}", "logprob": (lp[i - 1000] if lp else -0.3 * (i - 1000)) - (twist if i == 1000 else 0.0)} for i in ids]
        top.append({"token": "token_id:7", "logprob": -0.01})        # the sampled token, not a label
        self._send(200, {"choices": [{"message": {"content": ""}, "logprobs": {"content": [
            {"token": "token_id:7", "logprob": -0.01, "top_logprobs": top}]}}],
            "usage": {"prompt_tokens": len(self.ids(text)) + n_img * (self.IMAGE_TOKENS - 1), "completion_tokens": 1}})


def serve(handler):
    srv = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), handler)
    srv.daemon_threads = True
    threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    return srv, f"http://127.0.0.1:{srv.server_address[1]}"


def post(url, body, path="/v1/systemone"):
    req = urllib.request.Request(url + path, data=json.dumps(body).encode(), headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as r:
            return r.status, json.loads(r.read())
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        return e.code, json.loads(e.read() or b"{}")


def softmax_t(lp, T=CFG["temperature"]):
    e = [math.exp(x / T) for x in lp]
    return [x / sum(e) for x in e]


OK_Q = {"type": "choice", "instructions": "x", "criteria": {"a": "b"}}


class ShimTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        FakeVLLM.reset()
        self.vsrv, vurl = serve(FakeVLLM)
        self.shim = S.Shim(S.Contract(copy.deepcopy(CFG), env={}), S.VLLM(vurl, CFG["model"]))
        self.ssrv, self.url = serve(S.make_handler(self.shim))

    def tearDown(self):
        self.ssrv.shutdown()
        self.vsrv.shutdown()

    def test_choice_prompt_and_probabilities(self):
        body = {"state": {"doc": "MOCK_LP=[-0.2,-1.9,-3.0]"}, "questions": {"decision": {
            "type": "choice", "instructions": "Which?", "criteria": {"x": "first", "y": "second", "z": "third"}}}}
        st, r = post(self.url, body)
        self.assertEqual(st, 200, r)
        a = r["answers"]["decision"]
        for k, p in zip("xyz", softmax_t([-0.2, -1.9, -3.0])):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(a["probabilities"][k], p, places=12)
        self.assertEqual(a["choice"], "x")
        self.assertEqual(r["usage"]["output_tokens"], 1)   # one label token per answered question
        text = FakeVLLM.prompts[-1]
        self.assertTrue(text.startswith("<|im_start|>system\n" + CFG["prompt"]["system"] + "<|im_end|>\n"))
        self.assertIn('<|im_start|>user\nrecord: {"doc":"MOCK_LP=[-0.2,-1.9,-3.0]"}\nquestion: Which?\noptions:\n'
                      'A) x: first\nB) y: second\nC) z: third<|im_end|>\n', text)
        self.assertTrue(text.endswith("<|im_start|>assistant\n<think>\n\n</think>\n\nAnswer:"))

    def test_yes_no_floor_and_threshold(self):
        lp = [math.log(0.55), math.log(0.45)]
        q = {"type": "noul", "instructions": "Holds?", "criteria": {"true": "t", "false": "f"}, "threshold": 0.9}
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": f"MOCK_LP={json.dumps(lp)}", "questions": {"d": q}})
        a = r["answers"]["d"]
        self.assertEqual((st, a["noul"], a["decision"]), (200, CFG["noul_floor"], False))
        lp = [math.log(0.02), math.log(0.98)]        # committed: left alone
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": f"MOCK_LP={json.dumps(lp)}", "questions": {"d": q}})
        self.assertAlmostEqual(r["answers"]["d"]["noul"], softmax_t(lp)[0], places=12)

    def test_score_and_several_questions(self):
        qs = {"s": {"type": "score", "instructions": "rate", "criteria": ["lo", "mid", "hi"]},
              "c": {"type": "choice", "instructions": "pick", "criteria": {"a": "1", "b": "2"}},
              "n": {"type": "noul", "instructions": "ok?", "criteria": {"true": "y", "false": "n"}}}
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": "MOCK_LP=[-1.0,-0.5,-2.0]", "questions": qs})
        self.assertEqual((st, set(r["answers"]), FakeVLLM.completions), (200, {"s", "c", "n"}, 3))
        p = softmax_t([-1.0, -0.5, -2.0])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(r["answers"]["s"]["score"], p[1] + 2 * p[2], places=12)
        self.assertEqual(r["answers"]["s"]["legend"], {"0": "lo", "1": "mid", "2": "hi"})
        self.assertEqual(r["answers"]["c"]["choice"], "b")

    def test_prefix_cache_writes_and_no_tokenize_calls(self):
        """SHIM_CACHE_WRITE: "all" (default) sends no prefix_cache_write field (every read writes, stock behaviour);
        "first": of a multi-question one-way request only the first question writes (the others carry vllm_xargs
        prefix_cache_write false) and a single question writes nothing. The shared head is counted from the prompts'
        token ids returned with the reads: no /tokenize call."""
        qs = {"s": {"type": "score", "instructions": "rate", "criteria": ["lo", "mid", "hi"]},
              "c": {"type": "choice", "instructions": "pick", "criteria": {"a": "1", "b": "2"}},
              "n": {"type": "noul", "instructions": "ok?", "criteria": {"true": "y", "false": "n"}}}
        state = "MOCK_LP=[-1.0,-0.5,-2.0] " + " ".join(f"w{i}" for i in range(300))
        q_of = lambda t: next(q for q, x in qs.items() if f"question: {x['instructions']}" in t)
        st, _ = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": {"c": qs["c"]}})          # warms the label check
        self.assertEqual((st, FakeVLLM.writes[-1][1]), (200, None))
        FakeVLLM.writes, FakeVLLM.tokenizes = [], 0
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": qs})
        self.assertEqual((st, FakeVLLM.tokenizes), (200, 0))
        self.assertEqual({q_of(t): w for t, w in FakeVLLM.writes}, {"s": None, "c": None, "n": None})
        with mock.patch.object(S, "CACHE_WRITE", "first"):
            FakeVLLM.writes = []
            st, r = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": qs})
            self.assertEqual({q_of(t): w for t, w in FakeVLLM.writes}, {"s": None, "c": False, "n": False})
            FakeVLLM.writes = []
            post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": {"c": qs["c"]}})
            self.assertEqual(FakeVLLM.writes[-1][1], False)
            self.assertEqual(S.cache_writes(1), [False])
        self.assertEqual(S.cache_writes(3), [None, None, None])
        with mock.patch.object(S, "CACHE_WRITE", "none"):
            self.assertEqual(S.cache_writes(2), [False, False])

    def test_shared_head_without_returned_token_ids(self):
        """An engine that does not return prompt_token_ids: the shim falls back to /tokenize, same usage."""
        qs = {"c": {"type": "choice", "instructions": "pick", "criteria": {"a": "1", "b": "2"}},
              "n": {"type": "noul", "instructions": "ok?", "criteria": {"true": "y", "false": "n"}}}
        state = "MOCK_LP=[-1.0,-0.5] " + " ".join(f"w{i}" for i in range(100))
        st, with_ids = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": qs})
        FakeVLLM.no_token_ids, FakeVLLM.tokenizes = True, 0
        st2, without = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": qs})
        self.assertEqual((st, st2), (200, 200))
        self.assertGreater(FakeVLLM.tokenizes, 0)
        for u in (with_ids["usage"], without["usage"]):
            u.pop("latency_ms")
        self.assertEqual(with_ids["usage"], without["usage"])
        self.assertEqual(with_ids["answers"], without["answers"])

    def test_usage_counts_the_shared_head_once(self):
        qs = {"s": {"type": "score", "instructions": "rate", "criteria": ["lo", "mid", "hi"]},
              "c": {"type": "choice", "instructions": "pick", "criteria": {"a": "1", "b": "2"}},
              "n": {"type": "noul", "instructions": "ok?", "criteria": {"true": "y", "false": "n"}}}
        state = "MOCK_LP=[-1.0,-0.5,-2.0] " + " ".join(f"w{i}" for i in range(300))
        st, one = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": {"c": qs["c"]}})     # one question: its whole prompt
        self.assertEqual((st, one["usage"]["input_tokens"]), (200, len(FakeVLLM.ids(FakeVLLM.prompts[-1]))))
        singles = {}
        for qid, q in qs.items():
            st, r = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": {qid: q}})
            singles[qid] = r["answers"][qid]
        FakeVLLM.prompts = []
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": qs})
        self.assertEqual(st, 200)
        self.assertEqual(json.dumps(r["answers"], sort_keys=True), json.dumps(singles, sort_keys=True))   # answers unchanged
        ids = [FakeVLLM.ids(t) for t in FakeVLLM.prompts]
        shared = S.common_prefix_len(ids)
        self.assertGreater(shared, 300)                   # the system prompt and the whole record
        self.assertEqual(r["usage"]["input_tokens"], shared + sum(len(x) - shared for x in ids))
        self.assertLess(r["usage"]["input_tokens"], sum(len(x) for x in ids))

    def test_common_prefix(self):
        self.assertEqual(S.common_prefix_len([[1, 2, 3], [1, 2, 4], [1, 2]]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(S.common_prefix_len([[1, 2, 3], [9, 2, 3]]), 0)      # differing prompts: the plain sum
        self.assertEqual(S.common_prefix_len([[1, 2, 3]]), 0)                 # one question: nothing shared
        self.assertEqual(S.common_prefix_len([[1, 2], [1, 2]]), 2)

    def test_many_options_are_read_in_chunks(self):
        lp = [-5.0] * 200
        lp[150] = -0.1
        q = {"type": "choice", "instructions": "wide", "criteria": {f"opt{i}": f"d{i}" for i in range(200)}}
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": f"MOCK_LP={json.dumps(lp)}", "questions": {"decision": q}})
        self.assertEqual((st, FakeVLLM.completions, r["answers"]["decision"]["choice"]), (200, 2, "opt150"))

    def test_long_state_keeps_head_and_tail(self):
        self.shim.c.max_state_tokens = 40
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": " ".join(f"w{i}" for i in range(100)), "questions": {"d": OK_Q}})
        text = FakeVLLM.prompts[-1]
        self.assertEqual(st, 200)
        self.assertIn("record: w0 w1", text)
        self.assertIn("\n...\n", text)
        self.assertIn(" w99\nquestion: x", text)
        self.assertNotIn(" w50 ", text)

    def test_kept_alive_connection_has_no_delayed_ack_stall(self):
        # one client connection, many requests (as a client with keep-alive sends them): every reply must arrive
        # promptly, not ~40 ms late (Nagle on the reply's second write vs the client's delayed ACK)
        import http.client, time, urllib.parse
        u = urllib.parse.urlsplit(self.url)
        c = http.client.HTTPConnection(u.hostname, u.port, timeout=10)
        body = json.dumps({"state": "s", "questions": {"d": OK_Q}}).encode()
        times = []
        for _ in range(12):
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            c.request("POST", "/v1/systemone", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
            r = c.getresponse(); r.read()
            self.assertEqual(r.status, 200)
            times.append(time.perf_counter() - t0)
        c.close()
        times.sort()
        self.assertLess(times[len(times) // 2], 0.02, times)

    def test_status_codes(self):
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "questions": {}})[0], 400)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "questions": {"d": OK_Q}, "effort": "maximim"})[0], 400)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"questions": {"d": OK_Q}})[0], 422)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "questions": {"d": {"type": "rank", "criteria": {"a": 1}}}})[0], 422)
        wide = {"type": "choice", "instructions": "x", "criteria": {f"o{i}": "d" for i in range(256)}}
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "questions": {"d": wide}})[0], 422)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "MOCK_OVERFLOW", "questions": {"d": OK_Q}})[0], 422)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "MOCK_500", "questions": {"d": OK_Q}})[0], 502)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "questions": {"d": OK_Q}}, "/api/alpha/decisions")[0], 200)

    def test_health_is_not_ready_until_the_warm_up_file_exists(self):
        """SHIM_READY_FILE (serve.sh sets it): /health is 503 with "warming": true until the flag file exists, while
        /health?live=1 (the launcher's own check) and decisions already answer; once the file exists, 200 ok."""
        import tempfile
        flag = os.path.join(tempfile.mkdtemp(), "warmed")
        with mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"SHIM_READY_FILE": flag}):
            with self.assertRaises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as e:
                urllib.request.urlopen(self.url + "/health", timeout=30)
            self.assertEqual(e.exception.code, 503)
            h = json.loads(e.exception.read())
            self.assertEqual((h["ok"], h.get("warming")), (False, True))
            with urllib.request.urlopen(self.url + "/health?live=1", timeout=30) as r:
                self.assertTrue(json.loads(r.read())["ok"])
            for not_live in ("/health?live=10", "/health?notlive=1"):
                with self.assertRaises(urllib.error.HTTPError) as e2:
                    urllib.request.urlopen(self.url + not_live, timeout=30)
                self.assertEqual(e2.exception.code, 503, not_live)
            self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "questions": {"d": OK_Q}})[0], 200)   # the warm-up itself
            open(flag, "w").close()
            with urllib.request.urlopen(self.url + "/health", timeout=30) as r:
                h = json.loads(r.read())
            self.assertTrue(h["ok"])
            self.assertNotIn("warming", h)

    def test_health_and_a_misconfigured_backend(self):
        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.url + "/health", timeout=30) as r:
            h = json.loads(r.read())
        self.assertTrue(h["ok"])
        self.assertEqual(h["noul_floor"]["value"], CFG["noul_floor"])
        self.assertEqual(h["temperature_by_type"]["noul"], CFG["temperature"])
        bad = copy.deepcopy(CFG)
        bad["labels"][3] = "X Y"                      # two tokens: not a label
        shim = S.Shim(S.Contract(bad, env={}), self.shim.v)
        with self.assertRaises(S.UpstreamError) as e:
            shim.check_backend()
        self.assertEqual(e.exception.status, 503)
        wrong = S.Shim(S.Contract(dict(copy.deepcopy(CFG), model="other"), env={}), S.VLLM(self.shim.v.base, "other"))
        with self.assertRaises(S.UpstreamError) as e:
            wrong.check_backend()
        self.assertEqual(e.exception.status, 503)

    def test_temperature_by_type(self):
        cfg = dict(copy.deepcopy(CFG), temperature={"choice": 0.75, "noul": 1.3, "score": 0.65})
        shim = S.Shim(S.Contract(cfg, env={}), self.shim.v)
        srv, url = serve(S.make_handler(shim))
        self.addCleanup(srv.shutdown)
        lp = [-0.2, -1.9, -3.0]
        qs = {"c": {"type": "choice", "instructions": "Which?", "criteria": {"x": "first", "y": "second", "z": "third"}},
              "s": {"type": "score", "instructions": "rate", "criteria": ["lo", "mid", "hi"]},
              "n": {"type": "noul", "instructions": "ok?", "criteria": {"true": "y", "false": "n"}}}
        st, r = post(url, {"state": f"MOCK_LP={json.dumps(lp)}", "questions": qs})
        self.assertEqual(st, 200)
        for k, x in zip("xyz", softmax_t(lp, 0.75)):
            self.assertAlmostEqual(r["answers"]["c"]["probabilities"][k], x, places=12)
        ps = softmax_t(lp, 0.65)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(r["answers"]["s"]["score"], ps[1] + 2 * ps[2], places=12)
        py = softmax_t(lp[:2], 1.3)[0]                     # the yes/no read at T 1.3, then the floor
        self.assertAlmostEqual(r["answers"]["n"]["noul"], CFG["noul_floor"] if max(py, 1 - py) < CFG["noul_floor"] else py,
                               places=12)
        self.assertEqual(shim.health()["temperature_by_type"], {"choice": 0.75, "noul": 1.3, "score": 0.65})
        one = S.Contract(cfg, env={"SHIM_TEMPERATURE": "1"})
        self.assertEqual(one.temperature_by_type, {"choice": 1.0, "noul": 1.0, "score": 1.0})
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            S.Contract(dict(copy.deepcopy(CFG), temperature={"choice": 0.7, "noul": 1.0}), env={})
        self.assertEqual(self.shim.c.temperature_by_type, {t: CFG["temperature"] for t in S.PRIMITIVES})

    def test_a_dead_backend_is_a_502(self):
        self.vsrv.shutdown()
        self.vsrv.server_close()
        dead = S.Shim(S.Contract(copy.deepcopy(CFG), env={}), S.VLLM(self.shim.v.base, CFG["model"]))
        srv, url = serve(S.make_handler(dead))
        try:
            self.assertEqual(post(url, {"state": "s", "questions": {"d": OK_Q}})[0], 502)
        finally:
            srv.shutdown()
        self.vsrv, _ = serve(FakeVLLM)                # for tearDown


def png_bytes(w=2, h=1):
    """A valid w x h grey PNG, made with the standard library."""
    import struct
    import zlib

    def chunk(t, d):
        return struct.pack(">I", len(d)) + t + d + struct.pack(">I", zlib.crc32(t + d) & 0xffffffff)
    raw = b"".join(b"\x00" + b"\x80" * w for _ in range(h))
    return (b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" + chunk(b"IHDR", struct.pack(">IIBBBBB", w, h, 8, 0, 0, 0, 0))
            + chunk(b"IDAT", zlib.compress(raw)) + chunk(b"IEND", b""))


PNG = png_bytes()
PNG_B64 = S.base64.b64encode(PNG).decode()
PNG_URI = "data:image/png;base64," + PNG_B64
JPEG = b"\xff\xd8\xff\xe0" + b"\x00\x10JFIF" + bytes(range(64))
JPEG_B64 = S.base64.b64encode(JPEG).decode()
ICFG = dict(copy.deepcopy(CFG), image={"max_images": 4, "max_pixels": 1048576, "temperature": 0.9})
Q3 = {"type": "choice", "instructions": "Which?", "criteria": {"x": "first", "y": "second", "z": "third"}}


class ImageTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """The image path (serve_config.json with an "image" block), and the text path left as it was."""

    def setUp(self):
        FakeVLLM.reset()
        self.vsrv, vurl = serve(FakeVLLM)
        self.shim = S.Shim(S.Contract(copy.deepcopy(ICFG), env={}), S.VLLM(vurl, CFG["model"]))
        self.ssrv, self.url = serve(S.make_handler(self.shim))

    def tearDown(self):
        self.ssrv.shutdown()
        self.vsrv.shutdown()

    def user_text(self, req):
        return req["messages"][1]["content"][-1]["text"]

    def image_urls(self, req):
        return [p["image_url"]["url"] for p in req["messages"][1]["content"] if p["type"] == "image_url"]

    def test_text_requests_take_the_text_path_unchanged(self):
        body = {"state": {"doc": "MOCK_LP=[-0.2,-1.9,-3.0]", "link": "https://example.com/a.png",
                          "kind": {"type": "image", "image": "a sunset over the bay"}, "code": "Qk" + "A" * 80},
                "questions": {"decision": Q3}}
        st, r = post(self.url, body)
        self.assertEqual((st, FakeVLLM.chats, FakeVLLM.completions), (200, [], 1))
        plain = S.Shim(S.Contract(copy.deepcopy(CFG), env={}), self.shim.v)   # the same request, a text deployment
        prompt_img_cfg = FakeVLLM.prompts[-1]
        self.assertEqual(json.dumps(plain.decide(copy.deepcopy(body))["answers"], sort_keys=True),
                         json.dumps(r["answers"], sort_keys=True))
        self.assertEqual(FakeVLLM.prompts[-1], prompt_img_cfg)
        self.assertIn('"link":"https://example.com/a.png"', prompt_img_cfg)
        self.assertIn('"kind":{"type":"image","image":"a sunset over the bay"}', prompt_img_cfg)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.url + "/health", timeout=30) as h:
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(h.read())["images"]["max_images"], 4)

    def test_a_text_deployment_ignores_images(self):
        plain = S.Shim(S.Contract(copy.deepcopy(CFG), env={}), self.shim.v)
        plain.decide({"state": {"photo": PNG_URI, "n": "MOCK_LP=[-0.2,-1.9,-3.0]"}, "questions": {"d": Q3}})
        self.assertEqual(FakeVLLM.chats, [])
        self.assertIn(PNG_B64, FakeVLLM.prompts[-1])           # read as text, as before
        self.assertNotIn("images", plain.health())

    def test_data_uri_in_state(self):
        body = {"state": {"photo": PNG_URI, "note": "MOCK_LP=[-0.2,-1.9,-3.0]"}, "questions": {"decision": Q3}}
        st, r = post(self.url, body)
        self.assertEqual((st, FakeVLLM.completions, len(FakeVLLM.chats)), (200, 0, 1), r)
        req = FakeVLLM.chats[0]
        self.assertEqual(req["messages"][0], {"role": "system", "content": CFG["prompt"]["system"]})
        self.assertEqual(req["messages"][2], {"role": "assistant", "content": CFG["prompt"]["prefill"]})
        self.assertEqual(self.image_urls(req), [PNG_URI])
        self.assertEqual(req["mm_processor_kwargs"], {"max_pixels": 1048576})
        self.assertEqual(self.user_text(req), 'record: {"photo":"[image 1]","note":"MOCK_LP=[-0.2,-1.9,-3.0]"}\n'
                                              'question: Which?\noptions:\nA) x: first\nB) y: second\nC) z: third')
        a = r["answers"]["decision"]
        for k, p in zip("xyz", softmax_t([-0.2, -1.9, -3.0], T=0.9)):        # the image temperature
            self.assertAlmostEqual(a["probabilities"][k], p, places=12)
        self.assertEqual((a["choice"], r["usage"]["images"]), ("x", 1))
        # the chat render is the text prompt with the image slot ahead of the user text
        text_prompt = S.render_chat(CFG["prompt"]["system"], self.user_text(req), "Answer:")
        self.assertEqual(FakeVLLM.render(req["messages"]),
                         text_prompt.replace("<|im_start|>user\n", "<|im_start|>user\n" + S.VISION_SLOT))
        self.assertTrue(self.shim.health()["images"]["verified"])

    # an image link is fetched by the shim itself (safe fetch) and reaches vLLM as a data URI
    @mock.patch.object(S, "_fetch_bytes", lambda url, max_bytes=None: base64.b64decode(JPEG_B64))
    def test_every_image_form(self):
        state = {"a": [{"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": PNG_URI}}, "keep me"],
                 "b": PNG_B64, "c": {"type": "input_image", "image_url": "data:image/jpeg;base64," + JPEG_B64},
                 "d": "MOCK_LP=[-0.2,-1.9,-3.0]"}
        body = {"state": state, "questions": {"decision": Q3},
                "images": [PNG_URI, "https://example.com/x.jpg", {"url": PNG_B64}],
                "image_data": {"type": "base64", "media_type": "image/jpeg", "data": JPEG_B64}}
        self.shim.c.max_images = 8
        st, r = post(self.url, body)
        self.assertEqual(st, 200, r)
        req = FakeVLLM.chats[0]
        jpeg_uri = "data:image/jpeg;base64," + JPEG_B64
        self.assertEqual(self.image_urls(req), [PNG_URI, jpeg_uri, PNG_URI, jpeg_uri,
                                                PNG_URI, PNG_URI, jpeg_uri])
        self.assertIn('record: {"a":["[image 5]","keep me"],"b":"[image 6]","c":"[image 7]",', self.user_text(req))
        self.assertEqual(r["usage"]["images"], 7)

    def test_multipart(self):
        boundary = "b0undary"
        req = json.dumps({"state": {"listing": {"color": "red"}, "n": "MOCK_LP=[-0.2,-1.9,-3.0]"},
                          "questions": {"decision": Q3}}).encode()
        parts = [(b'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="request"\r\nContent-Type: application/json', req),
                 (b'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="image"; filename="p.png"\r\nContent-Type: image/png', PNG)]
        data = b"".join(b"--" + boundary.encode() + b"\r\n" + h + b"\r\n\r\n" + d + b"\r\n" for h, d in parts)
        data += b"--" + boundary.encode() + b"--\r\n"
        r = urllib.request.Request(self.url + "/v1/systemone", data=data,
                                   headers={"Content-Type": f"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(r, timeout=30) as resp:
            out = json.loads(resp.read())
        self.assertEqual(out["answers"]["decision"]["choice"], "x")
        self.assertEqual(self.image_urls(FakeVLLM.chats[0]), [PNG_URI])

    def test_limits_and_errors(self):
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": {"p": PNG_URI}, "images": [PNG_URI] * 4,
                                         "questions": {"d": Q3}})[0], 422)            # 5 images, at most 4
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": {"p": "data:image/png;base64,@@not base64@@"},
                                         "questions": {"d": Q3}})[0], 422)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "images": ["just words"], "questions": {"d": Q3}})[0], 422)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "images": [7], "questions": {"d": Q3}})[0], 422)
        self.assertEqual(FakeVLLM.chats, [])
        # an image vLLM cannot fetch is one bad item (422), not a backend failure (502)
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "s", "images": ["https://example.com/MOCK_422.png"],
                                         "questions": {"d": Q3}})[0], 422)

    def test_usage_counts_the_image_and_record_once(self):
        qs = {"c": Q3, "n": {"type": "noul", "instructions": "ok?", "criteria": {"true": "y", "false": "n"}},
              "s": {"type": "score", "instructions": "rate", "criteria": ["lo", "mid", "hi"]}}
        state = {"photo": PNG_URI, "words": " ".join(f"w{i}" for i in range(200))}
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": qs})
        self.assertEqual((st, len(FakeVLLM.chats)), (200, 3))
        whole = [len(FakeVLLM.ids(FakeVLLM.render(c["messages"]))) + FakeVLLM.IMAGE_TOKENS - 1 for c in FakeVLLM.chats]
        shared = S.common_prefix_len([FakeVLLM.ids(FakeVLLM.render(c["messages"])) for c in FakeVLLM.chats])
        self.assertGreater(shared, 200)
        self.assertEqual(r["usage"]["input_tokens"], sum(whole) - 2 * (shared + FakeVLLM.IMAGE_TOKENS - 1))
        st, one = post(self.url, {"state": state, "questions": {"c": Q3}})
        last = FakeVLLM.chats[-1]["messages"]
        self.assertEqual(one["usage"]["input_tokens"], len(FakeVLLM.ids(FakeVLLM.render(last))) + FakeVLLM.IMAGE_TOKENS - 1)

    def test_many_options_are_read_in_chunks(self):
        lp = [-5.0] * 200
        lp[150] = -0.1
        q = {"type": "choice", "instructions": "wide", "criteria": {f"opt{i}": f"d{i}" for i in range(200)}}
        st, r = post(self.url, {"state": {"p": PNG_URI, "m": f"MOCK_LP={json.dumps(lp)}"}, "questions": {"d": q}})
        self.assertEqual((st, len(FakeVLLM.chats), r["answers"]["d"]["choice"]), (200, 2, "opt150"))

    def test_a_template_that_is_not_the_models_is_a_503(self):
        FakeVLLM.skew = True
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": {"p": PNG_URI}, "questions": {"d": Q3}})[0], 503)
        self.assertEqual(FakeVLLM.chats, [])
        self.assertEqual(post(self.url, {"state": "text only", "questions": {"d": Q3}})[0], 200)   # text unaffected

    def test_lift_images(self):
        found = []
        state = {"s": "plain words", "n": 3, "l": ["x", {"y": None}], "u": "https://x.org/p.png",
                 "b64ish": "QUFB" * 40, "png": PNG_B64}
        out = S.lift_images(state, found)
        self.assertEqual(out["png"], "[image 1]")
        self.assertEqual(found, [PNG_URI])
        for k in ("s", "n", "l", "u", "b64ish"):
            self.assertIs(out[k], state[k])
        untouched = {"a": ["b", {"c": "d"}], "e": "A" * 100}
        self.assertIs(S.lift_images(untouched, []), untouched)
        self.assertEqual(S.sniff_image(b"RIFF\x00\x00\x00\x00WEBPVP8 "), "image/webp")
        self.assertEqual(S.extract_images({"state": "x"}), ("x", []))


class ServingFixesTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """A score's confidence is the probability of its most likely level; image decoding gives the same images without
    per-character scans; a kept-alive backend connection the server closed (or left idle) is replaced, a live one is
    reused."""

    @staticmethod
    def _png(w=80, h=60):
        import struct
        import zlib
